# REFACTOR-Rows — Alternative Set (Families 9–14, MIT/Apache 2.0)

Refactors moving from hardcoded patterns to config-driven engines (Genie DNA).
Format mirrors `new sets/REFACTOR-Rows.md` — Type A primarily, transition_type =
internal_improvement, B-side at 4.4 (refactor), P-side at 4.1 (feature/runtime
addition).

```
Common coverage labels (file-constant):
  source_license_state     : external_license_checked
  contract_signature       : 4.4 refactor
  pair_kind                : flow_node
  transition_type          : internal_improvement
```

---
